Conclusion: UDE 旨在建立一个标准化的基准测试生态系统，通过提供开源工具和脚本，助力研究人员更便捷、可靠地评估交互式 AI 系统，从而推动该领域的未来突破。

Abstract: Benchmarking AI systems in multi-turn interactive scenarios is essential for understanding their practical capabilities in real-world applications. However, existing evaluation protocols are highly heterogeneous, differing significantly in dataset formats, model interfaces, and evaluation pipelines, which severely impedes systematic comparison. In this work, we present UniDial-EvalKit (UDE), a unified evaluation toolkit for assessing interactive AI systems. The core contribution of UDE lies in its holistic unification: it standardizes heterogeneous data formats into a universal schema, streamlines complex evaluation pipelines through a modular architecture, and aligns metric calculations under a consistent scoring interface. It also supports efficient large-scale evaluation through parallel generation and scoring, as well as checkpoint-based caching to eliminate redundant computation. Validated across diverse multi-turn benchmarks, UDE not only guarantees high reproducibility through standardized workflows and transparent logging, but also significantly improves evaluation efficiency and extensibility. We make the complete toolkit and evaluation scripts publicly available to foster a standardized benchmarking ecosystem and accelerate future breakthroughs in interactive AI.

### [39] [ImplicitRM: Unbiased Reward Modeling from Implicit Preference Data for LLM alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23184)
*Hao Wang,Haocheng Yang,Licheng Pan,Lei Shen,Xiaoxi Li,Yinuo Wang,Zhichao Chen,Yuan Lu,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ImplicitRM，旨在解决从低成本隐式反馈中学习奖励模型时常见的负样本缺失和用户偏好偏差挑战，通过样本分层和无偏目标函数实现了高效的奖励建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的奖励建模高度依赖高成本的人工反馈数据。而隐式反馈（如点击、复制）虽然成本低，但面临缺乏明确负样本以及用户偏好偏差（不同回复获得反馈的倾向性不同）的挑战。

Method: 提出 ImplicitRM 框架，首先通过分层模型将训练样本划分为四个潜在组，随后基于似然最大化推导出理论上无偏的学习目标，以应对缺乏负样本和用户偏好偏差的问题。

Result: 实验证明，ImplicitRM 能够从隐式偏好数据中学习到准确的奖励模型，并有效解决了样本缺失和偏差问题。

Conclusion: ImplicitRM 为 RLHF 提供了一种具有成本效益的替代方案，能够在不依赖显式标注的情况下，从真实用户行为中学习高质量的奖励模型。

Abstract: Reward modeling represents a long-standing challenge in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) for aligning language models. Current reward modeling is heavily contingent upon experimental feedback data with high collection costs. In this work, we study \textit{implicit reward modeling} -- learning reward models from implicit human feedback (e.g., clicks and copies) -- as a cost-effective alternative. We identify two fundamental challenges in implicit reward modeling: (1) Implicit preference data lacks definitive negative samples, which makes standard positive-negative classification methods inapplicable; (2) Implicit preference data suffers from user preference bias, where different responses have different propensities to elicit user feedback actions, which exacerbates the difficulty of distinguishing definitive negative samples. To address these challenges, we propose ImplicitRM, which aims to learn unbiased reward models from implicit preference data. ImplicitRM stratifies training samples into four latent groups via a stratification model. Building on this, it derives a learning objective through likelihood maximization, which we prove is theoretically unbiased, effectively resolving both challenges. Experiments demonstrate that ImplicitRM learns accurate reward models across implicit preference datasets. Code is available on our project website.

</details>


### [40] [Decoding AI Authorship: Can LLMs Truly Mimic Human Style Across Literature and Politics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23219)
*Nasser A Alsadhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了GPT-4o等顶尖大模型模拟特定名家风格的能力，发现尽管AI能模仿语法复杂度，但在情感深度和风格多变性上仍逊于人类，其生成的文本极易通过困惑度等特征被识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI模拟人类特定风格能力的增强，研究者旨在探究顶尖LLM（如GPT-4o, Gemini 1.5 Pro, Claude 3.5）在模拟著名文学和政治人物（如惠特曼、特朗普等）风格时的真实效力。

Method: 采用零样本提示（Zero-shot prompting）生成合成语料库，并结合Transformer分类器（BERT）和可解释机器学习（XGBoost）进行评估。利用LIWC标记、困惑度（Perplexity）和可读性指数等特征进行多维度分析。

Result: AI生成的模仿文本具有高度可检测性。XGBoost模型仅靠8个刻画语言风格的特征即可达到高准确率。其中，困惑度是核心判别指标，揭示了AI输出在随机规律性上与人类写作（具有更高多变性）的显著差异。

Conclusion: 目前的LLM尚无法完全复制人类作者的情感密度和风格变化。这种统计学上的差异为数字人文和社交媒体中的作者身份鉴定提供了重要的基准和判别依据。

Abstract: Amidst the rising capabilities of generative AI to mimic specific human styles, this study investigates the ability of state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), including GPT-4o, Gemini 1.5 Pro, and Claude Sonnet 3.5, to emulate the authorial signatures of prominent literary and political figures: Walt Whitman, William Wordsworth, Donald Trump, and Barack Obama. Utilizing a zero-shot prompting framework with strict thematic alignment, we generated synthetic corpora evaluated through a complementary framework combining transformer-based classification (BERT) and interpretable machine learning (XGBoost). Our methodology integrates Linguistic Inquiry and Word Count (LIWC) markers, perplexity, and readability indices to assess the divergence between AI-generated and human-authored text. Results demonstrate that AI-generated mimicry remains highly detectable, with XGBoost models trained on a restricted set of eight stylometric features achieving accuracy comparable to high-dimensional neural classifiers. Feature importance analyses identify perplexity as the primary discriminative metric, revealing a significant divergence in the stochastic regularity of AI outputs compared to the higher variability of human writing. While LLMs exhibit distributional convergence with human authors on low-dimensional heuristic features, such as syntactic complexity and readability, they do not yet fully replicate the nuanced affective density and stylistic variance inherent in the human-authored corpus. By isolating the specific statistical gaps in current generative mimicry, this study provides a comprehensive benchmark for LLM stylistic behavior and offers critical insights for authorship attribution in the digital humanities and social media.

</details>


### [41] [I Came, I Saw, I Explained: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs on Figurative Meaning in Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23229)
*Shijia Zhou,Saif M. Mohammad,Barbara Plank,Diego Frassinelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了 8 种大模型解读梗图比喻义的能力，发现模型普遍存在过度解读的偏见，且其解释往往貌似合理实则脱离实际。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网梗图（memes）是流行的多模态交流方式，常含比喻意义，但目前尚不清楚多模态大模型（MLLMs）能否有效结合图文信息并准确解读其中的深层含义。

Method: 通过 3 个数据集评估了 8 种最先进生成式 MLLM 在检测和解释 6 种比喻含义方面的表现，并结合人工评估来分析模型生成解释的合理性与忠实度。

Result: 所有受试模型都表现出强烈的“倾向于将梗图关联到比喻含义”的偏见（即便梗图本身并无此意）；定性分析显示，即使模型给出了正确预测，其生成的解释也往往与原文内容不符。

Conclusion: MLLM 在识别梗图隐藏含义方面仍面临巨大挑战，主要体现为过度解读的偏见以及模型推理过程与结果之间的不一致性。未来研究需关注提升模型在多模态理解中的忠实度和推理准确度。

Abstract: Internet memes represent a popular form of multimodal online communication and often use figurative elements to convey layered meaning through the combination of text and images. However, it remains largely unclear how multimodal large language models (MLLMs) combine and interpret visual and textual information to identify figurative meaning in memes. To address this gap, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art generative MLLMs across three datasets on their ability to detect and explain six types of figurative meaning. In addition, we conduct a human evaluation of the explanations generated by these MLLMs, assessing whether the provided reasoning supports the predicted label and whether it remains faithful to the original meme content. Our findings indicate that all models exhibit a strong bias to associate a meme with figurative meaning, even when no such meaning is present. Qualitative analysis further shows that correct predictions are not always accompanied by faithful explanations.

</details>


### [42] [Is AI Catching Up to Human Expression? Exploring Emotion, Personality, Authorship, and Linguistic Style in English and Arabic with Six Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23251)
*Nasser A Alsadhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM模拟人类情感与性格的能力，发现AI生成的文本在情感信号编码上与人类存在本质区别，虽然AI文本易于被检测，但在低资源语言中，AI合成数据能显著增强性格分类器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨LLM在不同语言（英语和阿拉伯语）和文化背景下，模拟复杂人类特质（如情感表达和性格标记）的能力，特别是填补阿拉伯语等低资源语言在这一领域的动力研究空白。

Method: 研究对比了Jais、Mistral、LLaMA、GPT-4o、Gemini和DeepSeek六种模型。通过两项核心任务进行评估：1) 使用机器学习分类器区分人类与AI创作的文本；2) 交叉实验分类器在人类与AI数据上的迁移表现，并结合语言学及心理语言学分析其差异。

Result: 1) AI文本极易被识别（F1>0.95），但重写会降低识别率；2) 存在显著的泛化鸿沟，在人类数据上训练的分类器在AI文本上表现不佳，反之亦然；3) GPT-4o和Gemini在情感一致性上表现更优；4) AI生成的数据能有效提升阿拉伯语性格分类的任务性能。

Conclusion: LLM在刻画人类情感与性格方面仍与真人存在显著可测量的差异。研究强调了在低资源语言（如阿拉伯语）中使用合成数据增强分类器的潜力，同时也揭示了生成式AI检测和情感对齐在跨语言环境下的复杂性。

Abstract: The advancing fluency of LLMs raises important questions about their ability to emulate complex human traits, including emotional expression and personality, across diverse linguistic and cultural contexts. This study investigates whether LLMs can convincingly mimic emotional nuance in English and personality markers in Arabic, a critical under-resourced language with unique linguistic and cultural characteristics. We conduct two tasks across six models:Jais, Mistral, LLaMA, GPT-4o, Gemini, and DeepSeek. First, we evaluate whether machine classifiers can reliably distinguish between human-authored and AI-generated texts. Second, we assess the extent to which LLM-generated texts exhibit emotional or personality traits comparable to those of humans. Our results demonstrate that AI-generated texts are distinguishable from human-authored ones (F1>0.95), though classification performance deteriorates on paraphrased samples, indicating a reliance on superficial stylistic cues. Emotion and personality classification experiments reveal significant generalization gaps: classifiers trained on human data perform poorly on AI-generated texts and vice versa, suggesting LLMs encode affective signals differently from humans. Importantly, augmenting training with AI-generated data enhances performance in the Arabic personality classification task, highlighting the potential of synthetic data to address challenges in under-resourced languages. Model-specific analyses show that GPT-4o and Gemini exhibit superior affective coherence. Linguistic and psycholinguistic analyses reveal measurable divergences in tone, authenticity, and textual complexity between human and AI texts. These findings have implications for affective computing, authorship attribution, and responsible AI deployment, particularly within underresourced language contexts where generative AI detection and alignment pose unique challenges.

</details>


### [43] [Steering LLMs for Culturally Localized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23301)
*Simran Khanuja,Hongbin Liu,Shujian Zhang,John Lambert,Mingqing Chen,Rajiv Mathews,Lun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机械解释性的文化嵌入（CuE）方法，通过操纵 LLM 内部特征来诊断和引导其文化表现，证明了该方法能有效提取模型中的长尾文化知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 文化本地化方法（如提示词或对齐）多为黑盒，难以控制且无法区分模型是“缺乏知识”还是“无法唤起知识”，且模型往往偏向数据丰富的文化。

Method: 利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）识别编码文化信息的特征，将其聚合为“文化嵌入（CuE）”，并以此进行白盒干预（Steering）来引导模型输出。

Result: CuE 指导的方法显著提高了文化忠实度，并能挖掘出比提示词方法更罕见、长尾的文化概念；该方法与黑盒方法互补，可进一步提升本地化效果。

Conclusion: LLM 内部蕴含着比表现出来的更多的长尾文化知识，通过机械解释性方法（CuE）可以比单纯的提示词工程更有效地挖掘和控制这些知识。

Abstract: LLMs are deployed globally, yet produce responses biased towards cultures with abundant training data. Existing cultural localization approaches such as prompting or post-training alignment are black-box, hard to control, and do not reveal whether failures reflect missing knowledge or poor elicitation. In this paper, we address these gaps using mechanistic interpretability to uncover and manipulate cultural representations in LLMs. Leveraging sparse autoencoders, we identify interpretable features that encode culturally salient information and aggregate them into Cultural Embeddings (CuE). We use CuE both to analyze implicit cultural biases under underspecified prompts and to construct white-box steering interventions. Across multiple models, we show that CuE-based steering increases cultural faithfulness and elicits significantly rarer, long-tail cultural concepts than prompting alone. Notably, CuE-based steering is complementary to black-box localization methods, offering gains when applied on top of prompt-augmented inputs. This also suggests that models do benefit from better elicitation strategies, and don't necessarily lack long-tail knowledge representation, though this varies across cultures. Our results provide both diagnostic insight into cultural representations in LLMs and a controllable method to steer towards desired cultures.

</details>


### [44] [WISTERIA: Weak Implicit Signal-based Temporal Relation Extraction with Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23319)
*Duy Dao Do,Anaïs Halftermeyer,Thi-Bich-Hanh Dao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 WISTERIA 框架，通过特定的 Top-K 注意力池化机制来识别和利用文本中隐含的时间信号，从而提升了时间关系抽取的性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力模型通常关注全局显著的 Token，而忽略了决定特定事件对时间关系的局部、对特定对敏感的暗示性线索。

Method: 提出 WISTERIA 框架，结合多头注意力机制与针对事件对的 Top-K 池化策略，隔离并提取每一对事件中最具信息量的上下文 Token。

Result: 在 TimeBank-Dense、MATRES、TDDMan 和 TDDAuto 四个数据集上均取得了极具竞争力的准确率，并能识别出符合语言学逻辑的时间推理依据。

Conclusion: WISTERIA 为时间关系抽取提供了一个局部且可解释的视角，证明了通过 Top-K 注意力机制可以有效捕获支持时间推理的语言学线索。

Abstract: Temporal Relation Extraction (TRE) requires identifying how two events or temporal expressions are related in time. Existing attention-based models often highlight globally salient tokens but overlook the pair-specific cues that actually determine the temporal relation. We propose WISTERIA (Weak Implicit Signal-based Temporal Relation Extraction with Attention), a framework that examines whether the top-K attention components conditioned on each event pair truly encode interpretable evidence for temporal classification. Unlike prior works assuming explicit markers such as before, after, or when, WISTERIA considers signals as any lexical, syntactic, or morphological element implicitly expressing temporal order. By combining multi-head attention with pair-conditioned top-K pooling, the model isolates the most informative contextual tokens for each pair. We conduct extensive experiments on TimeBank-Dense, MATRES, TDDMan, and TDDAuto, including linguistic analyses of top-K tokens. Results show that WISTERIA achieves competitive accuracy and reveals pair-level rationales aligned with temporal linguistic cues, offering a localized and interpretable view of temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [45] [Failure of contextual invariance in gender inference with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23485)
*Sagar Kumar,Ariel Flint,Luca Maria Aiello,Andrea Baronchelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现 LLM 的性别推断极易受到无关语境（如句子中出现的其他无关代词）的影响，违反了上下文不变性，使得传统的偏见测试结果变得不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估 LLM 输出是否在上下文等效的任务表述下保持稳定。由于目前的偏见基准测试通常在去语境化（decontextualized）的设置下进行，研究者希望探讨非相关语境特征（如无关词汇）是否会干扰模型的性别推断。

Method: 通过一个受控的代词选择任务（pronoun selection task）进行测试。研究者引入了理论上不含信息的微小语境变化（如无关参照物的性别代词），并采用“默认语境性”（Contextuality-by-Default）分析框架来量化模型输出对语境的依赖程度。

Result: 1. 即使是非常微小的语境变化也会诱发模型输出的大幅系统性转移；2. 在有语境的情况下，模型与文化性别偏见的关联会变弱或消失，转而受无关特征影响（如无关代词的性别）；3. 19%--52% 的案例显示，这种依赖性无法归因于简单的代词重复。

Conclusion: LLM 在处理性别推断任务时存在严重的“上下文不稳定性”。这种对非信息性上下文的过度敏感，不仅增加了偏见评估的难度，也对其在医疗、法律等高风险领域的部署提出了安全性质疑。

Abstract: Standard evaluation practices assume that large language model (LLM) outputs are stable under contextually equivalent formulations of a task. Here, we test this assumption in the setting of gender inference. Using a controlled pronoun selection task, we introduce minimal, theoretically uninformative discourse context and find that this induces large, systematic shifts in model outputs. Correlations with cultural gender stereotypes, present in decontextualized settings, weaken or disappear once context is introduced, while theoretically irrelevant features, such as the gender of a pronoun for an unrelated referent, become the most informative predictors of model behaviour. A Contextuality-by-Default analysis reveals that, in 19--52\% of cases across models, this dependence persists after accounting for all marginal effects of context on individual outputs and cannot be attributed to simple pronoun repetition. These findings show that LLM outputs violate contextual invariance even under near-identical syntactic formulations, with implications for bias benchmarking and deployment in high-stakes settings.

</details>

### [46] [Unified Algebraic Absorption of Finite-Blocklength Penalties via Generalized Logarithmic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22358)
*Hiroki Suyari*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出利用 $q$-代数框架替代传统的 Edgeworth 展开来分析信道编码的有限块长极限，通过动态参数缩放将高阶非高斯修正项直接吸收进信息密度定义中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的有限块长信道编码限制评估依赖于正态近似和 Edgeworth 展开，需处理复杂的加性多项式修正项，缺乏统一的数学结构。

Method: 引入动态缩放法则 $1-q_n = \alpha n^{-1}$ 的广义 $q$-代数框架，将非高斯惩罚项（偏度等）吸收进 $q$-广义信息密度中，而非作为外部偏差项。

Result: 通过设置 $\alpha = T/(3V^2)$，该框架成功恢复了三阶编码极限（Third-order coding limit），且 $k$ 阶代数展开项与 $(k+1)$ 阶矩的 Edgeworth 修正项渐近阶数一致。

Conclusion: $q$-代数框架为有限块长信息论提供了一种新的数学表征方式，有效桥接了概论渐近分析与代数结构，能够更简洁地统一处理高阶统计波动。

Abstract: In finite-blocklength information theory, evaluating the fundamental limits of channel coding typically relies on normal approximations and Edgeworth expansions, which introduce additive polynomial corrections for skewness and higher-order moments. This paper proposes an alternative approach: rather than appending external error terms to a Gaussian baseline, we absorb these finite-length penalties using a generalized $q$-algebraic framework. By introducing a dynamic scaling law $1-q_n = αn^{-1}$ for the tuning parameter, we prove that the $q$-generalized information density corresponds to macroscopic higher-order fluctuations. Specifically, by setting this scaling constant to $α= T/(3V^2)$ (where $V$ is the varentropy and $T$ is the third central moment), our framework recovers the third-order coding limit, absorbing the $O(1)$ non-Gaussian penalty without relying on Hermite polynomials. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the $k$-th degree term of our algebraic expansion matches the $O(n^{1-k/2})$ asymptotic order of the $(k+1)$-th moment Edgeworth correction. This approach unifies classical probabilistic approximations within a single algebraic structure, establishing a mathematical connection between finite-blocklength analysis and generalized logarithmic mappings.

</details>


### [47] [Communication-Efficient Approximate Gradient Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22514)
*Sifat Munim,Aditya Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一系列通信高效的近似梯度编码方案，通过结构化矩阵和随机化技术平衡了通信成本与计算精度，并证明了其在分布式学习中的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式学习中，传统的梯度编码虽能解决“滞后节点”问题，但通信开销大。现有研究多关注精确恢复梯度，缺乏对通信高效且支持近似恢复梯度的系统化方案研究。

Method: 利用二分图、组合设计和强正则图构造结构化矩阵，结合随机化技术和代数约束，构造出允许工人发送小于 $d$ 长度向量的梯度编码方案。

Result: 推导出了近似误差的解析上限和最坏情况下的下限；证明了在合理的概率故障模型下，计算梯度的期望值等于真实梯度。数值实验验证了方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方案不仅提供了严谨的理论误差界限，还证明了在随机故障模型下的收敛性，是分布式大规模机器学习中缓解计算滞后和通信瓶颈的有效手段。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed learning aims at minimizing a loss function $L$ that depends on a training dataset with respect to a $d$-length parameter vector. The distributed cluster typically consists of a parameter server (PS) and multiple workers. Gradient coding is a technique that makes the learning process resilient to straggling workers. It introduces redundancy within the assignment of data points to the workers and uses coding theoretic ideas so that the PS can recover $\nabla L$ exactly or approximately, even in the presence of stragglers. Communication-efficient gradient coding allows the workers to communicate vectors of length smaller than $d$ to the PS, thus reducing the communication time. While there have been schemes that address the exact recovery of $\nabla L$ within communication-efficient gradient coding, to the best of our knowledge the approximate variant has not been considered in a systematic manner. In this work we present constructions of communication-efficient approximate gradient coding schemes. Our schemes use structured matrices that arise from bipartite graphs, combinatorial designs and strongly regular graphs, along with randomization and algebraic constraints. We derive analytical upper bounds on the approximation error of our schemes that are tight in certain cases. Moreover, we derive a corresponding worst-case lower bound on the approximation error of any scheme. For a large class of our methods, under reasonable probabilistic worker failure models, we show that the expected value of the computed gradient equals the true gradient. This in turn allows us to prove that the learning algorithm converges to a stationary point over the iterations. Numerical experiments corroborate our theoretical findings.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的能量检测（ED）方法假设噪声和信号遵循固定的概率分布，但在实际应用中，由于无线环境复杂和无线电技术多样，分布模型往往是不确定的，且信号变动剧烈。

Conclusion: 该模型通过引入非线性期望理论和G-正态分布，超越了传统概率方法的局限，为具有分布不确定性和信号多样性的认知无线电环境提供了稳健的理论框架。

Abstract: Classical energy detection (ED) methods for cognitive radio (CR) have addressed noise uncertainty as deviations in noise power and signal uncertainty as variability in signal characteristics, which use probabilistic methods and assume fixed probability distributions for both. In practical scenarios, due to the uncertainty in probability models and the significant variation of primary signals encountered by receivers across different radio technologies, wireless environments exhibit not only distributional uncertainty but also substantial signal variety. In this paper, we develop a generalized formulation of energy detection based on nonlinear expectation theory, where both the signal and noise distributions are uncertain. We utilize the $G$-normal distribution to characterize channel noise. Moreover, to capture practical signal variety, the absolute values of transmitted signal random variables are assumed to lie within a bounded range $[\underlineσ_X,\overlineσ_X]$. The worst-case detection performance is then characterized by a double supremum, meaning over all admissible distributions and all possible signal realizations. We derive estimations for the minimum and the maximum detection error probabilities, and demonstrate the validity of the results through numerical simulations. The proposed model generalizes the classical theoretical analysis of energy detection and offers a potential theoretical foundation for robust detection and information-theoretic analysis under distributional uncertainty.

</details>

*Paul Bezner,Erdem Eray Cil,Jannis Clausius,Oliver Griebel,Tim Janz,Lucas Johannsen,Claus Kestel,Felix Krieg,Haizheng Li,Jonathan Mandelbaum,Sisi Miao,Marvin Rübenacke,Daniel Tandler,Andreas Zunker,Laurent Schmalen,Norbert Wehn,Stephan ten Brink*

TL;DR: 本文探讨了 6G 演进中对统一信道编码方案的需求，旨在开发一种能兼顾高性能与低功耗硬件实现的通用编码技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 6G 的兴起，对更高数据速率的需求日益增长，而 5G 采用的 LDPC 码和极化码方案难以完全满足未来 6G 对低功耗硬件实现和统一编码架构的严苛要求。

Method: 通过分析各种 6G 通信场景，探讨并提出一种多功能且统一的编码方案构想，旨在克服现有方案的局限性。

Result: 确定了 6G 信道编码面临的新挑战，并为开发兼具极佳纠错性能与低复杂度编解码特性的新型方案提供了初步分析与研究方向。

Conclusion: 6G 需要一种能够平衡高性能、低复杂度和高能效的统一信道编码方案。

Abstract: The growing demand for higher data rates necessitates continuous innovations in wireless communication systems, particularly with the emergence of 6G. Channel coding plays a crucial role in this evolution. In 5G systems, rate-adaptive raptor-like quasi-cyclic irregular low-density parity-check codes are used for the data link, while polar codes with successive cancellation list decoding handle short messages on the synchronization channel. However, to meet the stringent requirements of future 6G systems, a versatile and unified coding scheme should be developed - one that offers competitive error-correcting performance alongside low complexity encoding and decoding schemes that enable energy-efficient hardware implementations. This white paper outlines the vision for such a unified coding scheme. We explore various 6G communication scenarios that pose new challenges to channel coding and provide a first analysis of potential solutions.

</details>

*Jialiang Fu,Wen-Xuan Lang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文基于次线性期望理论提出了一种新的大数定律，并将其应用于分析非独立同分布信号在通信反馈信道中的检测误差收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对通信系统中具有不确定性的非独立同分布（non-i.i.d.）信号，传统概率论的大数定律难以精确描述其收敛行为。

Method: 利用非线性期望（次线性期望）理论，推导出具有收敛率的新型大数定律，并将其应用于反馈信道检测模型的误差分析。

Result: 成功建立了一种新的带有收敛率的大数定律，并据此给出了通信检测错误上界概率的收敛速率估计。

Conclusion: 该定理能够有效评估通信系统中非独立同分布信号在反馈信道检测下的错误概率收敛界限。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a new law of large numbers with the rate of convergence for special partial sums in a probability space. The proof relies on nonlinear expectation theory, as the uncertainty of random variables in the special partial sums induces the sublinearity of the expectation. As an application, we apply the new theorem to analyze the feedback channel-based detection problem of non-i.i.d. input signals in communication systems. Specifically, we investigate the convergence rates of the upper probabilities of the detection errors within the sublinear expectation space.

</details>

*Minhao Zhu,Kaiming Shen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过利用低维子空间特性将大规模分布式天线系统的加权和速率最大化问题转化为黎曼流形优化问题，显著降低了计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有每集群功率约束的分布式天线系统中，由于天线部署密集导致波束赋形变量维度高，且多个独立功率约束增加了优化难度。

Method: 通过证明所有平稳点波束赋形向量均位于天线集群通道向量构成的子空间内，将原问题重构为椭圆流形积上的无约束优化问题，并开发了定制的黎曼共轭梯度算法。

Result: 数值模拟表明，该算法在达到与WMMSE和传统流形优化相同局部最优解的同时，具有更高的计算效率和扩展性，特别是在天线集群数量较多时优势显著。

Conclusion: 低维子空间特性不仅适用于总功率约束，同样适用于集群功率约束，为解决大规模分布式天线系统的计算瓶颈提供了理论支持。

Abstract: This paper addresses the weighted sum-rate (WSR) maximization problem in a downlink distributed antenna system subject to per-cluster power constraints. This optimization scenario presents significant challenges due to the high dimensionality of beamforming variables in dense antenna deployments and the structural complexity of multiple independent power constraints. To overcome these difficulties, we generalize the low-dimensional subspace property--previously established for sum-power constraints--to the per-cluster power constraint case. We prove that all stationary-point beamformers reside in a reduced subspace spanned by the channel vectors of the corresponding antenna cluster. Leveraging this property, we reformulate the original high-dimensional constrained problem into an unconstrained optimization task over a product of ellipsoidal manifolds, thereby achieving significant dimensionality reduction. We systematically derive the necessary Riemannian geometric structures for this specific manifold, including the tangent space, Riemannian metric, orthogonal projection, retraction, and vector transport. Subsequently, we develop a tailored Riemannian conjugate gradient algorithm to solve the reformulated problem. Numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed algorithm achieves the same local optima as standard benchmarks, such as the weighted minimum mean square error (WMMSE) method and conventional manifold optimization, but with substantially higher computational efficiency and scalability, particularly as the number of antenna clusters increases.

</details>


### [52] [Index-Based Scheduling for a Resource-Constrained Quantum Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23476)
*Subhankar Banerjee,Stavros Mitrolaris,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文利用RMAB理论和Whittle指数，针对受限内存的量子交换机设计了优化的多方纠缠调度策略，并引入AoEE指标来衡量性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化具有有限内存寄存器的量子交换机在服务多方纠缠请求时的效率，特别是解决平均服务率优化及纠缠建立的时效性问题。

Method: 将调度问题建模为休息型多臂老虎机（RMAB）框架，推导出Whittle指数的闭式解，并将内存受限的调度转化为0-1背包问题，通过动态规划及低复杂度贪婪算法求解。

Result: 成功获得所有请求-年龄对的Whittle指数闭式表达式，并开发了高效的内存约束调度策略。

Conclusion: 证明了调度问题具备指标性（indexability），且所提出的基于Whittle指数的算法在量子交换机调度中具有优越性。

Abstract: We consider a quantum switch with a finite number of quantum memory registers that aims to serve multipartite entanglement requests among $N$ users. We propose scheduling policies that aim to optimize the average number of requests served per unit time by efficiently utilizing the switch's available memory. To measure the performance of the scheduling policies, we employ the newly introduced metric of age of entanglement establishment (AoEE). We formulate the scheduling problem in a restless multi-armed bandit (RMAB) framework. We show that the scheduling of entanglement requests is indexable. Subsequently, we find a closed-form expression of the Whittle index for all possible request-age pairs. By modeling the Whittle index of each request as its reward and its cardinality as its cost, we formulate the memory-constrained scheduling problem as a $0$-$1$ knapsack problem and solve it via dynamic programming. Furthermore, we consider two low-complexity sequential greedy policies that leverage two different modified Whittle indices.

</details>

### [53] [Tied In on TikTok: Tie Strength and Emotional Dynamics in Algorithmic Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22504)
*Charles Bickham,Minh Duc Chu,Arianna Yuan,Valerie Lookingbill,Ehsan Mohammadi,Stuart Murray,Kristina Lerman,Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究证明了TikTok上的进食障碍（ED）话语存在类似社区的强连接动力学，用户通过频繁且情感互惠的互动形成了深层联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在算法驱动的短视频平台（如TikTok）上，用户间短促且分散的互动是否能形成真正的“强连接”社区，特别是针对进食障碍（ED）这类敏感话题。

Method: 分析了43,040个ED相关视频和超过56万条评论，并与非ED数据集对比。通过用户对（user-pair）的互动频率、情感消长和分类标注（康复、支持ED、经历分享）进行多维度量化分析。

Result: 互动频率与积极情感呈正相关，且在ED群体中更显著。互动起始的情感基调决定了后续走向：积极开场者保持积极，消极开场者难以转正。不同类型的ED内容显示出独特的情感互动模式。

Conclusion: 在短视频平台上，即便交互是碎片化的，也能形成具有社区特征的情感联系。这种关系受初始互动调性的影响极大，且在ED病患群体中表现出更强的内聚性和情感互惠。

Abstract: Whether genuine communities can form on algorithmically-driven short-form video platforms like TikTok remains an open question, given that user interactions are often brief, dispersed, and difficult to trace. Building on theories of tie strength and online community formation, we examine whether eating disorder (ED) discourse on TikTok exhibits behavioral and emotional signatures of strong ties, including more frequent, reciprocal, and affectively intense interactions. In this paper, we analyze 43,040 ED-related TikTok videos and over 560,000 comments, alongside a Non-ED comparison dataset. We find that at the user-pair level, greater interaction frequency is associated with increasingly positive emotional expression, a pattern that is amplified in ED-related conversations. This trend is also reflected linguistically, with pairs that interact more frequently exhibiting more of a positive tone. At the same time, how a relationship starts matters: pairs that begin with positive exchanges usually stay mostly positive as they continue interacting, while pairs that begin negatively may add some positive exchanges over time but rarely become mostly positive. To contextualize these dynamics, we classify ED videos into three content types (Pro-Recovery, Pro-ED, and ED Experiences) and find that each exhibits distinct emotional interaction patterns. These findings suggest that dense, emotionally structured relationships can emerge within ED discourse on TikTok. More broadly, our work provides one of the first empirical demonstrations of how community-like relational dynamics form and persist on algorithmically driven short-form video platforms.

</details>


### [54] [Gendered Communication Patterns of Political Elites on Truth Social](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23027)
*Tom Bidewell,Artemis Deligianni,Tuğrulcan Elmas,Clare Llewellyn,Björn Ross*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Truth Social 上的性别政治传播模式，发现主流平台的性别约束在该另类平台上依然存在，但女性精英会通过特定的修辞策略（如恐惧诉求）进行选择性适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关于性别对在线政治传播影响的研究多集中于主流平台（如 X），而对侧重极右翼、具有强烈男性霸权色彩的“替代科技”平台（如 Truth Social）中的性别动态研究较少。

Method: 采用了大规模定量分析，利用包含 129 位美国政治人物的 10.7 万条帖子的新数据集，分析其修辞风格、主题框架和受众互动。

Result: 女性政治精英表现出更多愉悦感、更少愤怒，且互动率显著更高；男女在保守议题的主题上相似，但在框架和策略上有差异，女性倾向使用更多基于恐惧的修辞，以适应平台性别规范。

Conclusion: 在 Truth Social 这种受极右翼意识形态影响的平台上，主流社交媒体中的性别约束依然存在，但会根据平台的党派取向和技术环境产生特定表达，揭示了性别、政治与替代性科技平台之间复杂的相互作用。

Abstract: The influence of gender on online political communication remains contested, with existing scholarship providing mixed evidence as to whether gender shapes political messaging in digital environments. However, this debate has largely centred on mainstream platforms such as X (formerly Twitter), leaving the dynamics of alt-tech social media underexamined. This paper addresses this gap by analysing gendered patterns of political communication on Truth Social, a hyper-partisan platform that functions as a hub for the most committed followers of the American far right, a community closely associated with hegemonic masculine norms. To address this gap, we present the first large-scale analysis of political elite communication on Truth Social, using a novel dataset of 107k posts from 129 U.S. political figures. We examine the extent to which gender influences rhetorical style, topic framing, and audience engagement. We find that many gendered communication patterns documented on mainstream platforms persist on Truth Social. In particular, women political elites tend to express more joy and less anger than men and receive significantly higher levels of audience engagement. At the same time, more nuanced differences emerge. Although men and women political elites discuss largely similar conservative themes, they differ in how these issues are framed and in the rhetorical strategies employed. Notably, posts associated with women political elites contain higher levels of fear-based rhetoric, potentially suggesting selective adaptation in communicative style to navigate gender norms on the platform. These findings suggest that on Truth Social, an alt-tech platform with distinct ideological characteristics, mainstream gendered constraints persist, but are expressed through platform-specific communicative patterns shaped by its partisan orientation and sociotechnical environment.

</details>

### [55] [Demystifying Low-Rank Knowledge Distillation in Large Language Models: Convergence, Generalization, and Information-Theoretic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22355)
*Alberlucia Rafael Soarez,Daniel Kim,Mariana Costa,Alejandro Torre*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文填补了低秩知识蒸馏的理论空白，证明了其收敛性与泛化能力，并提出了最优 rank 选择的数学准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管低秩知识蒸馏（如 LRC）在实证上取得了巨大成功，能以极低的计算开销实现高性能模型压缩，但其背后的理论基础（如收敛性、泛化能力及 rank 选择策略）尚不明确。

Method: 构建了一个严格的理论框架，涵盖了对低秩投影优化动力学的收敛性证明、泛化误差界的推导，以及基于信息论的激活克隆机制分析。

Result: 证明了低秩蒸馏具有 $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ 的收敛率；泛化误差与 rank 呈线性相关；并推导出最优 rank 理论值为 $r^* = O(\sqrt{n})$。实验结果与理论预测高度一致。

Conclusion: 该研究为低秩知识蒸馏提供了坚实的理论支撑，不仅证明了其收敛性，还给出了 rank 选择的最佳数学指导，对大语言模型的高效压缩具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation has emerged as a powerful technique for compressing large language models (LLMs) into efficient, deployable architectures while preserving their advanced capabilities. Recent advances in low-rank knowledge distillation, particularly methods like Low-Rank Clone (LRC), have demonstrated remarkable empirical success, achieving comparable performance to full-parameter distillation with significantly reduced training data and computational overhead. However, the theoretical foundations underlying these methods remain poorly understood. In this paper, we establish a rigorous theoretical framework for low-rank knowledge distillation in language models. We prove that under mild assumptions, low-rank projection preserves the optimization dynamics, yielding explicit convergence rates of $O(1/\sqrt{T})$. We derive generalization bounds that characterize the fundamental trade-off between model compression and generalization capability, showing that the generalization error scales with the rank parameter as $O(r(m+n)/\sqrt{n})$. Furthermore, we provide an information-theoretic analysis of the activation cloning mechanism, revealing its role in maximizing the mutual information between the teacher's and student's intermediate representations. Our theoretical results offer principled guidelines for rank selection, mathematically suggesting an optimal rank $r^* = O(\sqrt{n})$ where $n$ is the sample size. Experimental validation on standard language modeling benchmarks confirms our theoretical predictions, demonstrating that the empirical convergence, rank scaling, and generalization behaviors align closely with our bounds.

</details>


### [56] [SPDE Methods for Nonparametric Bayesian Posterior Contraction and Laplace Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22468)
*Enric Alberola-Boloix,Ioar Casado-Telletxea*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过 Langevin SPDE 框架，为无限维非参数贝叶斯模型推导了非渐近后验收缩率和有限样本 BvM 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决非参数贝叶斯模型在无限维设定下的后验收缩率 (PCR) 和有限样本 Bernstein-von Mises (BvM) 理论的推导难题。

Method: 将 Mou 等人 (2024) 的扩散理论扩展至无限维，利用可分希尔伯特空间上的 Langevin 随机偏微分方程 (SPDE) 来表征后验分布（作为不变测度），进而控制后验矩并建立量化拉普拉斯近似。

Result: 在多种似然曲率和正则性条件下，通过希尔伯特范数获得了非渐近集中率，并在一类非参数线性高斯逆问题中验证了该理论。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效处理无限维贝叶斯模型的集中度和拉普拉斯近似问题，为非参数推理提供了严格的理论支撑。

Abstract: We derive posterior contraction rates (PCRs) and finite-sample Bernstein von Mises (BvM) results for non-parametric Bayesian models by extending the diffusion-based framework of Mou et al. (2024) to the infinite-dimensional setting. The posterior is represented as the invariant measure of a Langevin stochastic partial differential equation (SPDE) on a separable Hilbert space, which allows us to control posterior moments and obtain non-asymptotic concentration rates in Hilbert norms under various likelihood curvature and regularity conditions. We also establish a quantitative Laplace approximation for the posterior. The theory is illustrated in a nonparametric linear Gaussian inverse problem.

</details>


### [57] [Privacy-Preserving Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback via Decoupled Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22563)
*Young Hyun Cho,Will Wei Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种针对RLHF的差分隐私微调框架，通过在奖励建模阶段引入隐私保护，在理论上证明了其最优性，并在实际对齐任务中取得了超越基准的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于偏好的微调（如RLHF）是训练大语言模型的关键，但所用数据包含敏感用户信息，迫切需要设计一种适应RLHF独特结构的差分隐私流水线。

Method: 提出一种隐私保护框架，仅在“奖励学习（Reward Learning）”阶段实施差分隐私（DP），然后利用生成的隐私奖励模型导出最终策略。

Result: 理论上确定了次优性间隙（suboptimality gap）及隐私代价；实验上在Gemma-2B-IT模型和Anthropic HH-RLHF数据集上验证了其性能优于现有的DP基准方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为偏好微调提供了理论支撑和实践指导，证明了在保证差分隐私的前提下，通过对奖励模型进行隐私处理可以实现高效的模型对齐。

Abstract: Preference-based fine-tuning has become an important component in training large language models, and the data used at this stage may contain sensitive user information. A central question is how to design a differentially private pipeline that is well suited to the distinct structure of reinforcement learning from human feedback. We propose a privacy-preserving framework that imposes differential privacy only on reward learning and derives the final policy from the resulting private reward model. Theoretically, we study the suboptimality gap and show that privacy contributes an additional additive term beyond the usual non-private statistical error. We also establish a minimax lower bound and show that the dominant term changes with sample size and privacy level, which in turn characterizes regimes in which the upper bound is rate-optimal up to logarithmic factors. Empirically, synthetic experiments confirm the scaling predicted by the theory, and experiments on the Anthropic HH-RLHF dataset using the Gemma-2B-IT model show stronger private alignment performance than existing differentially private baseline methods across privacy budgets.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨传统贝叶斯预测在不可知论情形下可能导致的过拟合及非零超额风险问题，并将 Zhu 和 Srebro 先前关于离散先验的工作扩展到连续先验和 PAC-Bayes 框架。

Method: 研究了一种 PAC-Bayes 学习规则，该规则在随机化预测器的训练误差与其相对于预设先验的 KL 散度之间进行权衡，并重点分析了缩放参数 $λ$ 对学习效果的影响。

Result: 证明了当 $λ=1$ 时（对应经验贝叶斯），可能出现持续的超额损失；而当 $λ \gg 1$（相当于使用与样本量相关的先验）时，即便在不可知论情况下也能确保一致消失的超额损失。同时精确刻画了正则化不足或过度的影响。

Conclusion: 在不可知论（agnostic）设置下，传统的贝叶斯学习规则（$λ=1$）并非最优风险最小化者；必须通过调节 $λ$ 来平衡先验与经验误差，以确保学习规则的泛化性。

Abstract: We consider a PAC-Bayes type learning rule for binary classification, balancing the training error of a randomized ''posterior'' predictor with its KL divergence to a pre-specified ''prior''. This can be seen as an extension of a modified two-part-code Minimum Description Length (MDL) learning rule, to continuous priors and randomized predictions. With a balancing parameter of $λ=1$ this learning rule recovers an (empirical) Bayes posterior and a modified variant recovers the profile posterior, linking with standard Bayesian prediction (up to the treatment of the single-parameter noise level). However, from a risk-minimization prediction perspective, this Bayesian predictor overfits and can lead to non-vanishing excess loss in the agnostic case. Instead a choice of $λ\gg 1$, which can be seen as using a sample-size-dependent-prior, ensures uniformly vanishing excess loss even in the agnostic case. We precisely characterize the effect of under-regularizing (and over-regularizing) as a function of the balance parameter $λ$, understanding the regimes in which this under-regularization is tempered or catastrophic. This work extends previous work by Zhu and Srebro [2025] that considered only discrete priors to PAC Bayes type learning rules and, through their rigorous Bayesian interpretation, to Bayesian prediction more generally.

</details>


### [59] [REALITrees: Rashomon Ensemble Active Learning for Interpretable Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22750)
*Simon D. Nguyen,Hayden McTavish,Kentaro Hoffman,Cynthia Rudin,Tyler H. McCormick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 REAL 框架，通过枚举所有近优模型（Rashomon Set）而非随机抽样来构建主动学习查询委员会，显著提升了标签效率和模型收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的主动学习框架（如 QBC）依赖随机特征子集或数据盲化产生多样性，这仅是认识不确定性的近似，缺乏对“所有可能成立的假设空间”的直接表征。

Method: 提出 REAL 框架。首先，详尽枚举所有近乎最优的模型（Rashomon Set）构成委员会；其次，利用 Gibbs 后验的 PAC-Bayesian 框架对成员加权以处理功能冗余；最后，在稀疏决策树上实现了精确枚举。

Result: REAL 在合成数据集和基准测试中均优于传统的随机集成方法。尤其在有噪声的环境中，它能更有效地利用模型多样性，实现更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: REAL 证明了基于候选假设空间（Rashomon Set）的确定性枚举比基于随机扰动的集成方法在主动学习中更有效。

Abstract: Active learning reduces labeling costs by selecting samples that maximize information gain. A dominant framework, Query-by-Committee (QBC), typically relies on perturbation-based diversity by inducing model disagreement through random feature subsetting or data blinding. While this approximates one notion of epistemic uncertainty, it sacrifices direct characterization of the plausible hypothesis space. We propose the complementary approach: Rashomon Ensembled Active Learning (REAL) which constructs a committee by exhaustively enumerating the Rashomon Set of all near-optimal models. To address functional redundancy within this set, we adopt a PAC-Bayesian framework using a Gibbs posterior to weight committee members by their empirical risk. Leveraging recent algorithmic advances, we exactly enumerate this set for the class of sparse decision trees. Across synthetic and established active learning baselines, REAL outperforms randomized ensembles, particularly in moderately noisy environments where it strategically leverages expanded model multiplicity to achieve faster convergence.

</details>

*Elisabeth Griesbauer,Leiv Rønneberg,Arnoldo Frigessi,Claudia Czado,Ingrid Hobæk Haff*

Main category: stat.ML

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的变分推理（VI）方法往往难以在计算效率与模型复杂依赖关系之间取得平衡，且传统的KL散度在恢复藤状共轭模型（Vine Copula）参数时存在局限性。

Method: 提出了一种逐步变分推理程序，通过Rényi散度定义证据下界（ELBO），并按藤结构的树序列逐层估计变分参数，同时引入了自动停止准则来控制模型复杂度。

Result: 该方法能够有效恢复更复杂的潜在依赖关系，在参数使用上更加精简，且在稀疏高斯过程等任务中的表现优于传统的均值场变分推理。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地在均值场变分推理（MFVI）与全潜在依赖模型之间建立了桥梁，其不仅模型表达能力强，而且参数效率高，尤其适用于稀疏高斯过程等复杂应用。

Abstract: We propose stepwise variational inference (VI) with vine copulas: a universal VI procedure that combines vine copulas with a novel stepwise estimation procedure of the variational parameters. Vine copulas consist of a nested sequence of trees built from copulas, where more complex latent dependence can be modeled with increasing number of trees. We propose to estimate the vine copula approximate posterior in a stepwise fashion, tree by tree along the vine structure. Further, we show that the usual backward Kullback-Leibler divergence cannot recover the correct parameters in the vine copula model, thus the evidence lower bound is defined based on the Rényi divergence. Finally, an intuitive stopping criterion for adding further trees to the vine eliminates the need to pre-define a complexity parameter of the variational distribution, as required for most other approaches. Thus, our method interpolates between mean-field VI (MFVI) and full latent dependence. In many applications, in particular sparse Gaussian processes, our method is parsimonious with parameters, while outperforming MFVI.

</details>

*Juan José Rodríguez-Aldavero,Juan José García-Ripoll*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出利用量子张量列（QTT）压缩特征函数，显著提升了独立随机变量加权和分布计算的效率，可处理超大规模离散化数据，并应用于风险价值（VaR）计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理非高斯分布随机变量加权和的概率分布时，传统方法面临维度爆炸和高计算量的问题，需寻找能实现指数级压缩的计算策略。

Method: 利用量子张量列（QTT）或矩阵乘积态（MPS）表示独立随机变量加权和的特征函数。利用其低秩结构特征，将全局特征函数分解为局部项进行处理。

Result: 在 Bernoulli 分布实验中，当分量 $D \gtrsim 300$ 时，键维（bond dimension）发生骤减，实现对数级时间和空间缩减；在对数正态分布实验中，处理频率模式达到 $2^{30}$，远超传统方法 $2^{24}$ 的上限。

Conclusion: QTT 格式为解决具有大量分量的非高斯分布随机变量求和问题提供了一种高效且具有可扩展性的新工具，对定量金融等领域具有重要意义。

Abstract: Characteristic functions of weighted sums of independent random variables exhibit low-rank structure in the quantized tensor train (QTT) representation, also known as matrix product states (MPS), enabling up to exponential compression of their fully non-Gaussian probability distributions. Under variable independence, the global characteristic function factorizes into local terms. Its low-rank QTT structure arises from intrinsic spectral smoothness in continuous models, or from spectral energy concentration as the number of components $D$ grows in discrete models. We demonstrate this on weighted sums of Bernoulli and lognormal random variables. In the former, despite an adversarial, incompressible small-$D$ regime, the characteristic function undergoes a sharp bond-dimension collapse for $D \gtrsim 300$ components, enabling polylogarithmic time and memory scaling. In the latter, the approach reaches high-resolution discretizations of $N = 2^{30}$ frequency modes on standard hardware, far beyond the $N = 2^{24}$ ceiling of dense implementations. These compressed representations enable efficient computation of Value at Risk (VaR) and Expected Shortfall (ES), supporting applications in quantitative finance and beyond.

</details>


### [62] [Between Resolution Collapse and Variance Inflation: Weighted Conformal Anomaly Detection in Low-Data Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23205)
*Oliver Hennhöfer,Christine Preisach*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续核密度估计的加权保形异常检测框架，解决了传统方法在分布偏移下因样本量缩减导致的检测效能损失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在处理非交换性数据（分布偏移）时，传统的加权保形异常检测面临最小p值与稳定性之间的权衡：权重越局部化，有效样本量越小，导致离散p值过于保守或由于平滑技术引入方差而掩盖异常。

Method: 提出一种连续推断松弛（continuous inference relaxation）方法，通过连续加权核密度估计（weighted kernel density estimation）将局部适应与尾部降解去耦。

Result: 实验证明该方法在离散基准方法无法检测出异常的情况下仍能保持检测能力，在保持边缘误差控制的同时，其统计功效（Power）显著优于标准方法。

Conclusion: 连续推断松弛方法解决了加权保形预测中精度与稳定性之间的矛盾，是处理非平稳数据异常检测的更有效工具。

Abstract: Standard conformal anomaly detection provides marginal finite-sample guarantees under the assumption of exchangeability . However, real-world data often exhibit distribution shifts, necessitating a weighted conformal approach to adapt to local non-stationarity. We show that this adaptation induces a critical trade-off between the minimum attainable p-value and its stability. As importance weights localize to relevant calibration instances, the effective sample size decreases. This can render standard conformal p-values overly conservative for effective error control, while the smoothing technique used to mitigate this issue introduces conditional variance, potentially masking anomalies. We propose a continuous inference relaxation that resolves this dilemma by decoupling local adaptation from tail resolution via continuous weighted kernel density estimation. While relaxing finite-sample exactness to asymptotic validity, our method eliminates Monte Carlo variability and recovers the statistical power lost to discretization. Empirical evaluations confirm that our approach not only restores detection capabilities where discrete baselines yield zero discoveries, but outperforms standard methods in statistical power while maintaining valid marginal error control in practice.

</details>


### [63] [Contextual Graph Matching with Correlated Gaussian Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23305)
*Mohammad Hassan Ahmad Yarandi,Luca Ganassali*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了Gaussian背景下具有节点特征的图匹配问题，首次严格表征了结构与上下文信息的交互如何影响恢复阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图匹配研究多关注单一结构信息，本文旨在探索当边权和节点特征同时存在关联时，如何刻画两种信息的交互对恢复性能的影响。

Method: 在Gaussian框架下研究上下文图匹配，利用信息论方法推导精确恢复（exact recovery）和几乎精确恢复（almost exact recovery）的理论阈值。

Result: 推导出了精确恢复的精确信息论阈值，并确定了几乎精确恢复的可行与不可行条件；发现在引入上下文信息后，不再观察到标准图匹配中的“全或无（all-or-nothing）”相变。

Conclusion: 结构信息（图边权）与上下文信息（节点特征）在图匹配中存在非平凡的交互作用，且其恢复阈值不再像标准图匹配那样重合。

Abstract: We investigate contextual graph matching in the Gaussian setting, where both edge weights and node features are correlated across two networks. We derive precise information-theoretic thresholds for exact recovery, and identify conditions under which almost exact recovery is possible or impossible, in terms of graph and feature correlation strengths, the number of nodes, and feature dimension. Interestingly, whereas an all-or-nothing phase transition is observed in the standard graph-matching scenario, the additional contextual information introduces a richer structure: thresholds for exact and almost exact recovery no longer coincide. Our results provide the first rigorous characterization of how structural and contextual information interact in graph matching, and establish a benchmark for designing efficient algorithms.

</details>

### [64] [Beyond Hard Constraints: Budget-Conditioned Reachability For Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22292)
*Janaka Chathuranga Brahmanage,Akshat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于安全条件可达集的离线安全强化学习方法，通过解耦奖励与成本约束，避免了传统方法中不稳定的对抗优化，实现了更稳健的安全策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实世界的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中，最大化奖励与满足安全约束（通常是冲突目标）往往会导致不稳定的极小极大（min/max）或对抗性优化。现有的可达性分析主要针对硬安全约束，较少涉及累积成本约束。

Method: 1. 定义“安全条件可达集”（Safety-Conditioned Reachability Set），将奖励最大化与累积安全成本约束解耦。2. 提出一种新型离线安全强化学习算法，通过该集合强制执行安全约束，无需环境交互。

Result: 在标准离线安全强化学习基准测试和真实海上航行任务中，该方法在保持安全性的同时，表现达到或超越了现有最先进（SOTA）的基准算法。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的基于可达性的离线强化学习框架，为处理带累积成本约束的安全关键型任务提供了一种比拉格朗日优化更稳定、高效的新途径。

Abstract: Sequential decision making using Markov Decision Process underpins many realworld applications. Both model-based and model free methods have achieved strong results in these settings. However, real-world tasks must balance reward maximization with safety constraints, often conflicting objectives, that can lead to unstable min/max, adversarial optimization. A promising alternative is safety reachability analysis, which precomputes a forward-invariant safe state, action set, ensuring that an agent starting inside this set remains safe indefinitely. Yet, most reachability based methods address only hard safety constraints, and little work extends reachability to cumulative cost constraints. To address this, first, we define a safetyconditioned reachability set that decouples reward maximization from cumulative safety cost constraints. Second, we show how this set enforces safety constraints without unstable min/max or Lagrangian optimization, yielding a novel offline safe RL algorithm that learns a safe policy from a fixed dataset without environment interaction. Finally, experiments on standard offline safe RL benchmarks, and a real world maritime navigation task demonstrate that our method matches or outperforms state of the art baselines while maintaining safety.

</details>

Abstract: Driver heterogeneity is often reduced to labels or discrete regimes, compressing what is inherently dynamic into static categories. Behavioral observations are embedded via non-linear Random Fourier Features, while state evolution blends temporal persistence of behavior with context-dependent profile activation. We evaluate our approach on empirical driving data, Third Generation Simulation Data (TGSIM), showing how driving profiles are extracted and analyzed.

Abstract: Synthetic Data Generation (SDG), leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs), has recently been recognized and broadly adopted as an effective approach to improve the performance of smaller but more resource and compute efficient LLMs through fine-tuning. A key challenge in SDG is ensuring the quality and diversity of the generated data. In this paper, we analyze the diversity and distribution of generated data in the embedding space, and demonstrate a strong correlation between the density of examples within a specific neighborhood and the accuracy of predictions on examples drawn from that region. Building on this insight, we present a targeted pipeline for embedding-based sampling that enhances data diversity and consistently improves performance across several benchmarks.

</details>


### [67] [COMPASS-Hedge: Learning Safely Without Knowing the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22348)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Manolis Vlatakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 COMPASS-Hedge 算法，首次在全信息在线学习中实现了对抗鲁棒性、随机实例最优性和基准安全性（Best-of-three-world）的完美兼顾，且无需预知环境参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习算法面临“三难困境”：难以同时在对抗性环境（最差情况）、随机环境（实例最优）以及针对固定基准的安全性（Baseline Safety）这三者之间取得平衡，且现有方法往往依赖先验参数或无法同时达到最优率。

Method: 引入了一种名为 COMPASS-Hedge 的算法，其核心是一个创新的自适应伪悔量缩放（adaptive pseudo-regret scaling）和基于阶段的激进策略（phase-based aggression），并结合了比较器感知混合策略（comparator-aware mixing strategy）。

Result: 该算法首次在全信息设定下同时实现了：1) 对抗环境下的 Minimax 最优悔量；2) 随机环境下的实例依赖最优悔量；3) 接近常数级（$ \tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1) $）的基准策略安全性。同时，该算法是无参数的（parameter-free）。

Conclusion: COMPASS-Hedge 证明了在全信息在线学习中，基准安全性（Baseline Safety）不需要以牺牲最坏情况下的鲁棒性或随机效率为代价，成功实现了三者性能的统一。

Abstract: Online learning algorithms often faces a fundamental trilemma: balancing regret guarantees between adversarial and stochastic settings and providing baseline safety against a fixed comparator. While existing methods excel in one or two of these regimes, they typically fail to unify all three without sacrificing optimal rates or requiring oracle access to problem-dependent parameters.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by introducing COMPASS-Hedge. Our algorithm is the first full-information method to simultaneously achieve: i) Minimax-optimal regret in adversarial environments; ii) Instance-optimal, gap-dependent regret in stochastic environments; and iii) $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$ regret relative to a designated baseline policy, up to logarithmic factors.
  Crucially, COMPASS-Hedge is parameter-free and requires no prior knowledge of the environment's nature or the magnitude of the stochastic sub optimality gaps. Our approach hinges on a novel integration of adaptive pseudo-regret scaling and phase-based aggression, coupled with a comparator-aware mixing strategy. To the best of our knowledge, this provides the first "best-of-three-world" guarantee in the full-information setting, establishing that baseline safety does not have to come at the cost of worst-case robustness or stochastic efficiency.

</details>


### [68] [Between the Layers Lies the Truth: Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs Using Intra-Layer Local Information Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22299)
*Zvi N. Badash,Yonatan Belinkov,Moti Freiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM跨层一致性模式的不确定性估计方法，相比传统探测方法更轻量、迁移性更强且在模型量化下更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常出现过度自信的错误。现有的基于输出的启发式方法不可靠，而基于内部表示的探测（probing）方法维度高、迁移性差且计算开销大。

Method: 提出一种紧凑的单实例UE方法，通过单次前向传播计算LLM内部表示的跨层一致性模式（cross-layer agreement patterns）进行评分。

Result: 在同分布测试中性能媲美探测法；在跨数据集迁移中表现更优（AUPRC提升最高2.86pts）；在4-bit量化下表现稳健，且能揭示不同模型编码不确定性的差异。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了层间一致性是量化LLM不确定性的一种有效、紧凑且具备鲁棒性的指标，为内部表示分析提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often confidently wrong, making reliable uncertainty estimation (UE) essential. Output-based heuristics are cheap but brittle, while probing internal representations is effective yet high-dimensional and hard to transfer.
  We propose a compact, per-instance UE method that scores cross-layer agreement patterns in internal representations using a single forward pass.
  Across three models, our method matches probing in-distribution, with mean diagonal differences of at most $-1.8$ AUPRC percentage points and $+4.9$ Brier score points. Under cross-dataset transfer, it consistently outperforms probing, achieving off-diagonal gains up to $+2.86$ AUPRC and $+21.02$ Brier points. Under 4-bit weight-only quantization, it remains robust, improving over probing by $+1.94$ AUPRC points and $+5.33$ Brier points on average.
  Beyond performance, examining specific layer--layer interactions reveals differences in how disparate models encode uncertainty. Altogether, our UE method offers a lightweight, compact means to capture transferable uncertainty in LLMs.

</details>


### [69] [Can Graph Foundation Models Generalize Over Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22984)
*Benjamin Gutteridge,Michael Bronstein,Xiaowen Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出固定架构限制了图基础模型的泛化能力，并提出一种在推理阶段动态调整图算子的框架，实现了跨域、跨任务的零样本架构自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨域图基础模型（GFMs）多采用固定的骨干架构，假设单一的消息传递机制能适配所有任务，忽略了不同任务对消息传递范围（Range）等架构属性的差异化需求。

Method: 提出一种无需重新训练的推理时适配框架，通过发现并混合任务特定的线性图算子（linear graph operators），动态调整推理过程中的GNN等效架构。

Result: 理论分析和实验表明，固定架构的GFM在处理与训练时需求不一致的任务时表现欠佳；所提框架在任意范围的合成任务和多个真实世界基准测试中，性能和鲁棒性均优于现有GFMs。

Conclusion: 图基础模型必须具备架构适应性才能实现真正的通用化，仅靠单一的固定架构（如固定层数的消去）无法应对现实世界中异构的任务需求。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) have recently attracted interest due to the promise of graph neural network (GNN) architectures that generalize zero-shot across graphs of arbitrary scales, feature dimensions, and domains. While existing work has demonstrated this ability empirically across diverse real-world benchmarks, these tasks share a crucial hidden limitation: they admit a narrow set of effective GNN architectures. In particular, current domain-agnostic GFMs rely on fixed architectural backbones, implicitly assuming that a single message-passing regime suffices across tasks. In this paper, we argue that architecture adaptivity is a necessary requirement for true GFMs. We show that existing approaches are non-robust to task-dependent architectural attributes and, as a case study, use range as a minimal and measurable axis along which this limitation becomes explicit. With theoretical analysis and controlled synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that fixed-backbone GFMs provably under-reach on tasks whose architectural requirements differ from those seen at training time. To address this issue, we introduce a framework that adapts effective GNN architecture at inference time by discovering and mixing task-specific linear graph operators, enabling zero-shot generalization across tasks with heterogeneous architectural requirements, without retraining. We validate our approach on arbitrary-range synthetic tasks and a suite of real-world benchmarks, demonstrating improved performance and robustness over existing domain-agnostic GFMs.

</details>


### [70] [Scaling Attention via Feature Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22300)
*Yan Xie,Tiansheng Wen,Tangda Huang,Bo Chen,Chenyu You,Stefanie Jegelka,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sparse Feature Attention (SFA)，通过特征维度的稀疏化而非序列维度的压缩，结合高效的 FlashSFA 算子，在不损失模型精度的前提下显著降低了注意力机制的计算成本和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 扩展到超长上下文受到自注意力机制 $O(n^2 d)$ 复杂度的限制，现有的序列维度优化方法往往会降低模型精度。

Method: 提出稀疏特征注意力（SFA），将查询（Q）和键（K）表示为 k-稀疏编码，并开发了 IO 感知的 FlashSFA 核函数，直接在稀疏重叠上操作而无需实例化稠密矩阵。

Result: 在 GPT-2 和 Qwen3 预训练中，SFA 在保持与稠密基线一致的精度的前提下，速度提升达 2.5倍，FLOPs 和 KV 缓存减少近 50%，并在长上下文检索任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: SFA 证明了特征轴上的稀疏性是实现高效注意力机制的一个尚未被充分探索且极具潜力的方向，能够以极小的质量损失实现超长上下文扩展。

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to ultra-long contexts is bottlenecked by the $O(n^2 d)$ cost of self-attention. Existing methods reduce this cost along the sequence axis through local windows, kernel approximations, or token-level sparsity, but these approaches consistently degrade accuracy. In this paper, we instead explore an orthogonal axis: feature sparsity. We propose Sparse Feature Attention (SFA), where queries and keys are represented as $k$-sparse codes that preserve high-dimensional expressivity while reducing the cost of attention from $Θ(n^2 d)$ to $Θ(n^2 k^2/d)$. To make this efficient at scale, we introduce FlashSFA, an IO-aware kernel that extends FlashAttention to operate directly on sparse overlaps without materializing dense score matrices. Across GPT-2 and Qwen3 pretraining, SFA matches dense baselines while improving speed by up to $2.5\times$ and reducing FLOPs and KV-cache by nearly 50\%. On synthetic and downstream benchmarks, SFA preserves retrieval accuracy and robustness at long contexts, outperforming short-embedding baselines that collapse feature diversity. These results establish feature-level sparsity as a complementary and underexplored axis for efficient attention, enabling Transformers to scale to orders-of-magnitude longer contexts with minimal quality loss. Code is available at https://github.com/YannX1e/Sparse-Feature-Attention.

</details>


### [71] [Latent Semantic Manifolds in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22301)
*Mohamed A. Mabrok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过黎曼几何框架解析了LLM连续状态与离散Token之间的转化，提出了表达间隙概念，并揭示了模型内部语义空间的通用缩放特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型隐藏层的连续向量计算与离散Token输出之间存在“几何失配”，这种离散化导致的语义扭曲及其对模型性能的影响尚未得到充分的理论解释。

Method: 开发了一个数学框架，将LLM隐藏状态视为Riemann流形上的点（配备Fisher信息度量），并利用Voronoi图划分语义空间；通过余面积公式（coarea formula）和率失真理论进行理论推导，并在6个Transformer架构（124M-1.5B参数）上进行实验验证。

Result: 证明了表达间隙（expressibility gap）随体积线性缩放的规律；发现不同模型具有通用的“沙漏型”本征维度分布和光滑曲率结构；观察到在不同规模模型中均存在持续的、靠近边界的表示“硬核”现象。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了LLM的离散输出受底层几何规律支配，通过控制隐藏层流形的参数映射，可以定量预测模型的语言建模能力和表达极限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform internal computations in continuous vector spaces yet produce discrete tokens -- a fundamental mismatch whose geometric consequences remain poorly understood. We develop a mathematical framework that interprets LLM hidden states as points on a latent semantic manifold: a Riemannian submanifold equipped with the Fisher information metric, where tokens correspond to Voronoi regions partitioning the manifold. We define the expressibility gap, a geometric measure of the semantic distortion from vocabulary discretization, and prove two theorems: a rate-distortion lower bound on distortion for any finite vocabulary, and a linear volume scaling law for the expressibility gap via the coarea formula. We validate these predictions across six transformer architectures (124M-1.5B parameters), confirming universal hourglass intrinsic dimension profiles, smooth curvature structure, and linear gap scaling with slopes 0.87-1.12 (R^2 > 0.985). The margin distribution across models reveals a persistent hard core of boundary-proximal representations invariant to scale, providing a geometric decomposition of perplexity. We discuss implications for architecture design, model compression, decoding strategies, and scaling laws

</details>


### [72] [Research on Individual Trait Clustering and Development Pathway Adaptation Based on the K-means Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22302)
*Qianru Wei,Jihaoyu Yang,Cheng Zhang,Jinming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用K-means聚类算法分析学生多维数据，实现精准的职业倾向分类，助力高校个性化就业指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有研究多侧重于职业路径预测，而缺乏对学生多维特征组合与具体职业方向匹配度研究的问题。

Method: 收集3000余名学生的CET-4成绩、GPA、性格特征及学生干部经历等数据，采用K-means聚类算法，通过最小化簇内平方误差将学生分为四个主要群体。

Result: 成功将学生划分为特征鲜明的四个群体，并证实不同特征组合的学生适合不同的职业方向；针对各群体提出了具有针对性的职业建议。

Conclusion: K-means算法能有效识别学生特征模式，为高校个性化职业指导提供科学依据，显著提升就业成功率。

Abstract: With the development of information technology, the application of artificial intelligence and machine learning in the field of education shows great potential. This study aims to explore how to utilize K-means clustering algorithm to provide accurate career guidance for college students. Existing methods mostly focus on the prediction of career paths, but there are fewer studies on the fitness of students with different combinations of characteristics in specific career directions. In this study, we analyze the data of more than 3000 students on their CET-4 scores, GPA, personality traits and student cadre experiences, and use the K-means clustering algorithm to classify the students into four main groups. The K-means clustering algorithm groups students with similar characteristics into one group by minimizing the intra-cluster squared error, ensuring that the students within the same cluster are highly similar in their characteristics, and that differences between different clusters are maximized. Based on the clustering results, targeted career guidance suggestions are provided for each group. The results of the study show that students with different combinations of characteristics are suitable for different career directions, which provides a scientific basis for personalized career guidance and effectively enhances students' employment success rate. Future research can further improve the precision of clustering and the guidance effect by expanding the sample size, increasing the feature variables and considering external factors.

</details>


### [73] [Sample Transform Cost-Based Training-Free Hallucination Detector for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22303)
*Zeyang Ding,Xinglin Hu,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用基于Wasserstein距离的分布复杂度指标（AvgWD和EigenWD）来检测大语言模型的幻觉，这是一种无需训练且适用于白盒与黑盒模型的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的幻觉问题阻碍了可靠部署，现有检测器需在准确性、轻量化和通用性之间平衡。作者认为模型响应分布的复杂度是幻觉的有效指标。

Method: 通过计算生成样本Token嵌入集之间的最优传输（Optimal-Transport）距离，构建Wasserstein距离矩阵，并由此导出AvgWD（平均成本）和EigenWD（成本复杂度）两个指标。

Result: 该方法实现了无须训练的幻觉检测，且在黑盒模型下也可通过“教师强制”扩展。实验证明其性能优于强不确定性基准。

Conclusion: 分布复杂度是衡量大语言模型真实性的有效信号，所提指标在模型和数据集之间具有互补性。

Abstract: Hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) remain a central obstacle to trustworthy deployment, motivating detectors that are accurate, lightweight, and broadly applicable. Since an LLM with a prompt defines a conditional distribution, we argue that the complexity of the distribution is an indicator of hallucination. However, the density of the distribution is unknown and the samples (i.e., responses generated for the prompt) are discrete distributions, which leads to a significant challenge in quantifying the complexity of the distribution. We propose to compute the optimal-transport distances between the sets of token embeddings of pairwise samples, which yields a Wasserstein distance matrix measuring the costs of transforming between the samples. This Wasserstein distance matrix provides a means to quantify the complexity of the distribution defined by the LLM with the prompt. Based on the Wasserstein distance matrix, we derive two complementary signals: AvgWD, measuring the average cost, and EigenWD, measuring the cost complexity. This leads to a training-free detector for hallucinations in LLMs. We further extend the framework to black-box LLMs via teacher forcing with an accessible teacher model. Experiments show that AvgWD and EigenWD are competitive with strong uncertainty baselines and provide complementary behavior across models and datasets, highlighting distribution complexity as an effective signal for LLM truthfulness.

</details>


### [74] [Mitigating Premature Discretization with Progressive Quantization for Robust Vector Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22304)
*Wenhao Zhao,Qiran Zou,Zhouhan Lin,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProVQ，通过将量化难度由易到难渐进式调整，解决了 VQ 训练中的过早离散化问题，在多模态生成和生物序列建模中均达到领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的向量量化（VQ）存在“过早离散化”（Premature Discretization）问题，即在编码器充分捕获数据流形之前就强制进行离散化，限制了模型的表示能力。

Method: 提出渐进式量化（Progressive Quantization, ProVQ），通过课程学习的方式，将量化过程从连续潜在空间平滑退火（Annealing）至离散空间。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K/100 图像重建与生成任务中取得显著提升；在蛋白质结构分词基准 StrutTokenBench 上刷新了性能上限。

Conclusion: ProVQ 是一种通用且高效的向量量化改进方案，不仅提升了传统图像生成模型的质量，还在蛋白质结构等科学建模领域表现出卓越的潜力。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) has become the cornerstone of tokenization for many multimodal Large Language Models and diffusion synthesis. However, existing VQ paradigms suffer from a fundamental conflict: they enforce discretization before the encoder has captured the underlying data manifold. We term this phenomenon Premature Discretization. To resolve this, we propose Progressive Quantization (ProVQ), which incorporates the dynamics of quantization hardness as a fundamental yet previously overlooked axis in VQ training. By treating quantization as a curriculum that smoothly anneals from a continuous latent space to a discrete one, ProVQ effectively guides the codebook toward the well-expanded manifolds. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the broad effectiveness of ProVQ across diverse modalities. We report improved reconstruction and generative performance on the ImageNet-1K and ImageNet-100 benchmarks, highlighting the ProVQ's boost for generative modeling. Furthermore, ProVQ proves highly effective for modeling complex biological sequences, establishing a new performance ceiling for protein structure tokenization on the StrutTokenBench leaderboard.

</details>


### [75] [CN-Buzz2Portfolio: A Chinese-Market Dataset and Benchmark for LLM-Based Macro and Sector Asset Allocation from Daily Trending Financial News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22305)
*Liyuan Chen,Shilong Li,Jiangpeng Yan,Shuoling Liu,Qiang Yang,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推出 CN-Buzz2Portfolio，一个针对中国市场的可复现金融智能体基准，通过“新闻到资产配置”的任务评估 LLM 处理宏观叙事与复杂决策的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 评估存在困境：实盘交易不可复现且存在结果偏差，而静态基准测试局限于个股选择，忽略了宏观市场关注度和复杂的决策逻辑。

Method: 提出了 CN-Buzz2Portfolio 基准测试和名为 CPA（压缩、感知、分配）的三阶段智能体工作流，通过模拟 2024 年至 2025 年的真实公共关注流，评估模型在 ETF 等大类资产上的配置能力。

Result: 对 9 种 LLM 的实验显示，不同模型在将宏观叙事转化为投资组合权重方面存在显著差异，揭示了通用推理与金融决策对齐程度的不同。

Conclusion: CN-Buzz2Portfolio 为金融智能体的研究提供了标准化的评估框架，强调了从宏观叙事到资产配置的推理能力，有助于推动可持续的金融 AI 研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly transitioning from static Natural Language Processing (NLP) tasks including sentiment analysis and event extraction to acting as dynamic decision-making agents in complex financial environments. However, the evolution of LLMs into autonomous financial agents faces a significant dilemma in evaluation paradigms. Direct live trading is irreproducible and prone to outcome bias by confounding luck with skill, whereas existing static benchmarks are often confined to entity-level stock picking and ignore broader market attention. To facilitate the rigorous analysis of these challenges, we introduce CN-Buzz2Portfolio, a reproducible benchmark grounded in the Chinese market that maps daily trending news to macro and sector asset allocation. Spanning a rolling horizon from 2024 to mid-2025, our dataset simulates a realistic public attention stream, requiring agents to distill investment logic from high-exposure narratives instead of pre-filtered entity news. We propose a Tri-Stage CPA Agent Workflow involving Compression, Perception, and Allocation to evaluate LLMs on broad asset classes such as Exchange Traded Funds (ETFs) rather than individual stocks, thereby reducing idiosyncratic volatility. Extensive experiments on nine LLMs reveal significant disparities in how models translate macro-level narratives into portfolio weights. This work provides new insights into the alignment between general reasoning and financial decision-making, and all data, codes, and experiments are released to promote sustainable financial agent research.

</details>


### [76] [UniFluids: Unified Neural Operator Learning with Conditional Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22309)
*Haosen Li,Qi Meng,Jiahao Li,Rui Zhang,Ruihua Song,Liang Ma,Zhi-Ming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniFluids 是首个基于 Flow-matching 的统一 PDE 算子学习框架，通过四维时空表示和并行生成技术，打破了多维度异构 PDE 模拟的速度与精度评价瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 PDE 基础模型多采用自回归模式，效率受限且难以统一处理不同维度和物理变量的复杂异构数据集。

Method: 提出 UniFluids 框架，基于 Flow-matching 和扩散 Transformer（DiT）实现并行序列生成。采用统一的四维时空表示处理异构数据，并利用 $x$-prediction 技术针对低维流形特性优化预测精度。

Result: 在 1D、2D 和 3D 的多个 PDE 数据集上表现出极高的预测精度，并展现出优异的可扩展性（Scalability）和跨场景泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniFluids 为统一偏微分方程算子学习提供了一个高效且可扩展的新范式，证明了 Flow-matching 结合 Transformer 在科学计算领域的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Partial differential equation (PDE) simulation holds extensive significance in scientific research. Currently, the integration of deep neural networks to learn solution operators of PDEs has introduced great potential. In this paper, we present UniFluids, a conditional flow-matching framework that harnesses the scalability of diffusion Transformer to unify learning of solution operators across diverse PDEs with varying dimensionality and physical variables. Unlike the autoregressive PDE foundation models, UniFluids adopts flow-matching to achieve parallel sequence generation, making it the first such approach for unified operator learning. Specifically, the introduction of a unified four-dimensional spatiotemporal representation for the heterogeneous PDE datasets enables joint training and conditional encoding. Furthermore, we find the effective dimension of the PDE dataset is much lower than its patch dimension. We thus employ $x$-prediction in the flow-matching operator learning, which is verified to significantly improve prediction accuracy. We conduct a large-scale evaluation of UniFluids on several PDE datasets covering spatial dimensions 1D, 2D and 3D. Experimental results show that UniFluids achieves strong prediction accuracy and demonstrates good scalability and cross-scenario generalization capability. The code will be released later.

</details>


### [77] [Bridging the Gap Between Climate Science and Machine Learning in Climate Model Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22320)
*Luca Schmidt,Nina Effenberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个整合气候科学与机器学习的框架，旨在通过开发易用、可靠且任务明确的仿真器，解决气候模型计算成本高及机器学习方法信任度低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候模型计算成本高昂且技术门槛高，而机器学习仿真器虽然能降低成本，但因可访问性低、专业知识缺乏及物理可信度受质疑而难以普及。

Method: 提出了一个整合气候科学与机器学习视角的通用框架，旨在开发易于采用、任务明确且具有物理可靠性的仿真器。

Result: 确定了克服应用障碍的关键路径，即通过演示仿真器的可靠性并满足特定的、定义明确的任务需求来建立信任。

Conclusion: 通过跨学科合作并优先考虑易用性与任务导向的设计，可以有效弥合机器学习与气候科学之间的鸿沟，加速气候决策支持。

Abstract: While climate models provide insights for climate decision-making, their use is constrained by significant computational and technical demands. Although machine learning (ML) emulators offer a way to bypass the high computational costs, their effective use remains challenging. The hurdles are diverse, ranging from limited accessibility and a lack of specialized knowledge to a general mistrust of ML methods that are perceived as insufficiently physical. Here, we introduce a framework to overcome these barriers by integrating both climate science and machine learning perspectives. We find that designing easy-to-adopt emulators that address a clearly defined task and demonstrating their reliability offers a promising path for bridging the gap between our two fields.

</details>


### [78] [A Multi-Modal CNN-LSTM Framework with Multi-Head Attention and Focal Loss for Real-Time Elderly Fall Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22313)
*Lijie Zhou,Luran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种集成多传感器融合与注意力机制的多模态深度学习框架，旨在实现高精度、低延迟的老年人实时跌倒检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对全球老龄化背景下，传统单模态跌倒检测误报率高以及机器学习方法对人工特征工程依赖性强的问题。

Method: 提出MultiModalFallDetector框架，结合多尺度CNN、多传感器数据融合（加速度计、陀螺仪、生理信号）、多头自注意力机制、Focal Loss损失函数、辅助任务正则化以及迁移学习技术。

Result: 在SisFall数据集上达到98.7的F1值、98.9的召回率和99.4的AUC-ROC；边缘设备推理延迟低于50ms，显著优于现有基准模型。

Conclusion: 该模型在保持极高准确率的同时兼顾了低延迟，证明了其在老年人护理及其实时监测系统中的应用价值。

Abstract: The increasing global aging population has intensified the demand for reliable health monitoring systems, particularly those capable of detecting critical events such as falls among elderly individuals. Traditional fall detection approaches relying on single-modality acceleration data suffer from high false alarm rates, while conventional machine learning methods require extensive hand-crafted feature engineering. This paper proposes a novel multi-modal deep learning framework, MultiModalFallDetector, designed for real-time elderly fall detection using wearable sensors. Our approach integrates multiple innovations: a multi-scale CNN-based feature extractor capturing motion dynamics at varying temporal resolutions; fusion of tri-axial accelerometer, gyroscope, and four-channel physiological signals; incorporation of a multi-head self-attention mechanism for dynamic temporal weighting; adoption of Focal Loss to mitigate severe class imbalance; introduction of an auxiliary activity classification task for regularization; and implementation of transfer learning from UCI HAR to SisFall dataset. Extensive experiments on the SisFall dataset, which includes real-world simulated fall trials from elderly participants (aged 60-85), demonstrate that our framework achieves an F1-score of 98. 7, Recall of 98. 9, and AUC-ROC of 99. 4, significantly outperforming baseline methods including traditional machine learning and standard deep learning approaches. The model maintains sub- 50ms inference latency on edge devices, confirming its suitability for real-time deployment in geriatric care settings.

</details>


### [79] [A Theoretical Framework for Energy-Aware Gradient Pruning in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22465)
*Emmanouil M. Athanasakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习提出了一种感知能耗的梯度剪枝方法 CWMP，通过权衡参数重要性与硬件成本，实现了更高效的通信与能量利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Top-K 梯度稀疏化方法仅考虑参数大小，忽略了不同硬件操作（如内存更新与数据传输）之间存在的能效差异。

Method: 提出成本权重幅度剪枝（CWMP），将剪枝过程建模为能量约束投影问题，根据参数更新的幅度与其物理成本的比率进行优先级排序。

Result: 在非独立同分布（non-IID）的 CIFAR-10 基准测试中，CWMP 在性能与能耗的帕累托前沿（Pareto frontier）上始终优于传统的 Top-K 基准。

Conclusion: CWMP 通过将硬件成本纳入剪枝决策，为边缘设备的联邦学习提供了一个更优的性能-能耗平衡方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is constrained by the communication and energy limitations of decentralized edge devices. While gradient sparsification via Top-K magnitude pruning effectively reduces the communication payload, it remains inherently energy-agnostic. It assumes all parameter updates incur identical downstream transmission and memory-update costs, ignoring hardware realities. We formalize the pruning process as an energy-constrained projection problem that accounts for the hardware-level disparities between memory-intensive and compute-efficient operations during the post-backpropagation phase. We propose Cost-Weighted Magnitude Pruning (CWMP), a selection rule that prioritizes parameter updates based on their magnitude relative to their physical cost. We demonstrate that CWMP is the optimal greedy solution to this constrained projection and provide a probabilistic analysis of its global energy efficiency. Numerical results on a non-IID CIFAR-10 benchmark show that CWMP consistently establishes a superior performance-energy Pareto frontier compared to the Top-K baseline.

</details>


### [80] [Problems with Chinchilla Approach 2: Systematic Biases in IsoFLOP Parabola Fits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22339)
*Eric Czech,Zhiwei Xu,Yael Elmatad,Yixin Wang,William Held*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了主流缩放定律拟合方法（Chinchilla Approach 2）的系统性偏差，并提出了一种基于变量投影的改进型 Approach 3，能更精准且高效地预测大模型的最优算力分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
